 -  Query identifier: `select ns.*, j.jobAsJson from(     select j.id, j.awaitingOn AS awaitedJobId, p.state AS awaitedJobState         FROM jobrunr_jobs j INNER JOIN jobrunr_jobs p ON p.id = j.awaitingOn         WHERE j.state = 'AWAITING' AND j.rateLimiter IS NULL AND (j.awaitingOnState = p.state OR p.state  IN ('SUCCEEDED','FAILED','DELETED','NOT_APPLICABLE'))  ORDER BY j.rateLimiter ASC, j.priority ASC, j.updatedAt ASC LIMIT :limit) ns, jobrunr_jobs j WHERE j.id = ns.id`

#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= :lastChildUpdatedAfter OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
 - Query with values: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= '2025-10-16T11:04:40.683+0000' OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
